# Copilot Primitives Catalog — Plugin Components

Lists every runtime component declared by every plugin as an individually discoverable item.

Part of the [Copilot primitives catalog](../github-copilot.md). Generated file: do not
hand-edit it. Regenerate with `python3 harness/github-copilot/scripts/generate_catalog.py`.

## Overview

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Primitive type | Plugin component |
| Entries | 919 |
| Canonical source | `harness/github-copilot/plugins/*/` |
| Typical use cases | Finding a specific agent, skill, hook, MCP or LSP server, output style, or client extension without browsing package trees. |

Qualified names use `plugin:item`. Shared library copies remain listed here to show plugin membership; their standalone source also appears on the matching primitive page.
